+# LR-01 — late-reading series and money limit
+
+Status: implementation-ready (Planner packet, 2026-10-15; owner selected `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` at 0.20% over `I-action-store`; implementation and independent review pending)
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — the Finding and Financials money panel gain owner-visible limit, end-reason and no-current-amount states. Owner walkthrough below is a merge checkpoint.
+Size: M, two phases; phase 2 review is the slice review. Branch for implementation: `task/LR-01-late-reading-series-and-money-limit`.
+Depends on: registry `I-S2-02`, `I-S3-01`, `I-S4-01`, `I-S4-02b`; built S4-02c consequence work and S4-02d schedule are the operational seam. This is a hard prerequisite of `I-site-overview-read` and schedule installation on demo/walkthrough hosts; real pilot additionally requires validated physical clock semantics and the other registry gates.
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+Resolve `.agent/S4-02c-review.md` F1 and `.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md` late-reading carries L-a–L-f, L1 and the S4-02b panel residual under decision `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`. Run 1 currently yields S3 `INDETERMINATE` / `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` / `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS`, a revised `SUPPORTED` Finding ending at 07:00 with only `OPEN_BOUND`, then `CURRENT PRICED` 7.5625 USD. The on-time-sequenced late upload yields 15.8125 USD. Neither figure is an automatic current-money claim when its generator series is unresolved. Do not rewrite the S4-02c fixture or its accepted consequence history.
+
+## Reviewed contract first — proposed S3 cohort v4
+
+Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
+
+1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
+3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.
+
+## Existing code and required changes
+

No Lab private truth as product proof. |

+
+**Money invariant:** `CURRENT PRICED` is necessary but insufficient. Current amount requires the current selected Finding/window to have no material unresolved generator-series limit, no pending reassessment/match, an exact consequence head for that FindingRevision, and the existing source/currentness checks. Other non-series limitations remain visible under the reviewed financial bound. The signal is computed on every read from persisted lineage, so a late evidence acceptance immediately withholds current money while work is pending. Do not rely on a UI-only predicate.
+

+
+**Owner walkthrough:** on a private Site/DB, show run 1 after late acceptance and tick: revised Finding with series limit and `SERIES_LIMIT` end; Financials has no current amount, links to the recorded 7.5625 USD revision and exact assessments. +
+## Architect questions — one batch, recommendations pending ruling
+
+1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
+2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
+3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
+4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.
+
+**Out of scope:** the broader owner analysis-input-data backlog (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2176-2178`) still owns per-analytic required inputs, cadence and coverage for simulated and real Sites at W1 exit; it must include clock attestation then. This packet closes the specific late-reading ordering and amount hazard. S4-02d I3 decided-at clock comparand remains its separate evidence-owner carry (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2223-2231`).

+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — the Finding and Financials money panel gain owner-visible limit, end-reason and no-current-amount states. Owner walkthrough below is a merge checkpoint.

+Depends on: registry `I-S2-02`, `I-S3-01`, `I-S4-01`, `I-S4-02b`; built S4-02c consequence work and S4-02d schedule are the operational seam. This is a hard prerequisite of `I-site-overview-read` and schedule installation on demo/walkthrough hosts; real pilot additionally requires validated physical clock semantics and the other registry gates.
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+Resolve `.agent/S4-02c-review.md` F1 and `.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md` late-reading carries L-a–L-f, L1 and the S4-02b panel residual under decision `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`. Run 1 currently yields S3 `INDETERMINATE` / `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` / `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS`, a revised `SUPPORTED` Finding ending at 07:00 with only `OPEN_BOUND`, then `CURRENT PRICED` 7.5625 USD. The on-time-sequenced late upload yields 15.8125 USD. Neither figure is an automatic current-money claim when its generator series is unresolved. Do not rewrite the S4-02c fixture or its accepted consequence history.
+
+## Reviewed contract first — proposed S3 cohort v4
+
+Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
+
+1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
+3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.
+
+## Existing code and required changes
+
+| Existing main anchor | Work in this packet |
+| --- | --- |
+| Gateway increments sequence on admission `gateway/assetops_gateway/service.py:8-11,649-655`; accepted rows contain `source_time`, `sequence_epoch`, `sequence`, `source_mode` but no clock attestation `backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:161-207`. | Add a versioned public clock-attestation record and a reviewed ingestion/fixture route (new migration/adapter/API or importer); preserve an explicit unavailable state for unvalidated real sources. No Lab private truth as product proof. |
+| `AcceptedReading`, `EvidenceQuery` and input digest `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assessment_inputs.py:70-136,205,334`; Postgres query maps current fields `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_assessment_store.py:48-56,123-136`. | Add attestation provenance/uncertainty to the accepted public analytic input and digest (new fields); avoid retroactively changing the bytes of old runs. |
+| Sequence inversion and overlap `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/cohort.py:113-136`; `dispatch-3.1` model/limits `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/dispatch_v3.py:39,528-550`. | Implement the reviewed cohort contract with a new model version (new model/module or versioned rules). Cover clock absent/expired/conflicting, overlap, epoch switch, run 1 and sequenced-on-time upload. Continue typed `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS` at window level where refused. |
+| `DispatchAssessmentWorker.reconcile` sees model staleness `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assessment_worker.py:173-185`; immutable `analytic_run_selection` `backend/migrations/versions/0008_assessment_selection.py:28-44`; late acceptance test `backend/tests/test_s3_dispatch_assessment_postgres.py:110-143`. | Queue every affected day and neighboring bracket/continuity window through the existing job path; include attestation changes, not just new accepted readings. Append new selections/revisions, preserve exact old IDs and history; idempotent replay and scoped tenant/Site checks. |
+| Matcher `MATCHER_VERSION` and supported write basis `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/finding_episode.py:66,435-479`; selected interval read has window limitations/jobs `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_assessment_store.py:559-579`; immutable `finding_revision` `backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:64-105`. | Revise the Finding on reassessment. If a generator-series refusal intersects the former/current episode or cuts its candidate tail, carry `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS` with span and linked assessment/evidence IDs into the new Finding revision. State `SERIES_LIMIT` as the episode end reason (new typed claim field), retaining `OPEN_BOUND` only when independently true. Keep supported candidate spans and historical revisions, but never imply the truncated tail was judged avoidable. |
+| Consequence bridge `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:279-320,347-365`, stored consequence read `backend/assetops_backend/financials/consequence_read.py:149-212`; current amount rendering `frontend/src/sites/FindingConsequence.tsx:369-436,526-562`. | Add a read-derived, versioned `money_eligibility` (new shared projection): `ELIGIBLE`, `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, or `PENDING_REASSESSMENT`, with exact FindingRevision, window assessment/run, source limit/evidence and consequence IDs. It must use the **latest selected window and latest Finding** for current claims; historical/pinned rows stay explicitly recorded/audit only. The S4-02b bridge and Finding money panel consume it: suppress the headline amount and all “current” labels on affected run 1 even if consequence currentness remains `CURRENT PRICED`; show the limit, truthful end reason and exact links. Do not delete or relabel stored 7.5625 USD history. S5-01 consumes this same signal, withholds the amount on both non-eligible states and shows the reason/links. |
+| Schedule seam `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/upstream.py:13,110`, health `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/health.py:152-203`, install gate `tools/schedule-install-gate.py:26-43`. | Wire pending S3/S4 work and stopped-worker/currentness state into eligibility; a stale selected window cannot become `ELIGIBLE`. Keep the installation gate closed until reviewed slice completion. S5-01 still owns its own read/UI integration. |
+
+**Money invariant:** `CURRENT PRICED` is necessary but insufficient. Current amount requires the current selected Finding/window to have no material unresolved generator-series limit, no pending reassessment/match, an exact consequence head for that FindingRevision, and the existing source/currentness checks. Other non-series limitations remain visible under the reviewed financial bound. The signal is computed on every read from persisted lineage, so a late evidence acceptance immediately withholds current money while work is pending. Do not rely on a UI-only predicate.
+

+| 2 — Finding and money surfaces | Build new matcher version, truthful series/end claim, shared eligibility and Financials panel. Focused `backend/tests/test_s4_finding_store_postgres.py`, `backend/tests/test_s4_consequence_read.py` and targeted Finding API/UI tests (new) prove suppressed current headline and exact historical linkage. Extend walkthrough with two modes (new switch/assertions) using `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py:111-126,214-343,365-391` and tick/no-hand-run path `tools/s402d_tick_walkthrough.py:94-146,180-375,386-414`; each uses its tool's private DB prefix and drop command. Assert exact S3 status/limits, Finding revision/end reason, consequence amount/IDs, eligibility, UI headline and S5 contract for both variants. +
+**Owner walkthrough:** on a private Site/DB, show run 1 after late acceptance and tick: revised Finding with series limit and `SERIES_LIMIT` end; Financials has no current amount, links to the recorded 7.5625 USD revision and exact assessments. Then show on-time-sequenced late upload with valid simulated clock attestation, supported full episode and 15.8125 USD current bound, and a real-source-without-clock case that stays limited/no current amount. Do not install the environment schedule on the owner's demo host before review; use the walkthrough's explicit tick.
+
+## Architect questions — one batch, recommendations pending ruling
+
+1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
+2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
+3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
+4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.
+
+**Out of scope:** the broader owner analysis-input-data backlog (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2176-2178`) still owns per-analytic required inputs, cadence and coverage for simulated and real Sites at W1 exit; it must include clock attestation then. This packet closes the specific late-reading ordering and amount hazard. S4-02d I3 decided-at clock comparand remains its separate evidence-owner carry (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2223-2231`).

+# LR-01 — late-reading series and money limit
+
+Status: implementation-ready (Planner packet, 2026-10-15; owner selected `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` at 0.20% over `I-action-store`; implementation and independent review pending)
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — the Finding and Financials money panel gain owner-visible limit, end-reason and no-current-amount states. Owner walkthrough below is a merge checkpoint.
+Size: M, two phases; phase 2 review is the slice review. Branch for implementation: `task/LR-01-late-reading-series-and-money-limit`.
+Depends on: registry `I-S2-02`, `I-S3-01`, `I-S4-01`, `I-S4-02b`; built S4-02c consequence work and S4-02d schedule are the operational seam. This is a hard prerequisite of `I-site-overview-read` and schedule installation on demo/walkthrough hosts; real pilot additionally requires validated physical clock semantics and the other registry gates.
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+Resolve `.agent/S4-02c-review.md` F1 and `.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md` late-reading carries L-a–L-f, L1 and the S4-02b panel residual under decision `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`. Run 1 currently yields S3 `INDETERMINATE` / `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` / `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS`, a revised `SUPPORTED` Finding ending at 07:00 with only `OPEN_BOUND`, then `CURRENT PRICED` 7.5625 USD. The on-time-sequenced late upload yields 15.8125 USD. Neither figure is an automatic current-money claim when its generator series is unresolved. Do not rewrite the S4-02c fixture or its accepted consequence history.
+
+## Reviewed contract first — proposed S3 cohort v4
+
+Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
+
+1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
+3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.
+
+## Existing code and required changes
+
+| Existing main anchor | Work in this packet |
+| --- | --- |
+| Gateway increments sequence on admission `gateway/assetops_gateway/service.py:8-11,649-655`; accepted rows contain `source_time`, `sequence_epoch`, `sequence`, `source_mode` but no clock attestation `backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:161-207`. | Add a versioned public clock-attestation record and a reviewed ingestion/fixture route (new migration/adapter/API or importer); preserve an explicit unavailable state for unvalidated real sources. No Lab private truth as product proof. |
+| `AcceptedReading`, `EvidenceQuery` and input digest `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assessment_inputs.py:70-136,205,334`; Postgres query maps current fields `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_assessment_store.py:48-56,123-136`. | Add attestation provenance/uncertainty to the accepted public analytic input and digest (new fields); avoid retroactively changing the bytes of old runs. |
+| Sequence inversion and overlap `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/cohort.py:113-136`; `dispatch-3.1` model/limits `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/dispatch_v3.py:39,528-550`. | Implement the reviewed cohort contract with a new model version (new model/module or versioned rules). Cover clock absent/expired/conflicting, overlap, epoch switch, run 1 and sequenced-on-time upload. Continue typed `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS` at window level where refused. |
+| `DispatchAssessmentWorker.reconcile` sees model staleness `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assessment_worker.py:173-185`; immutable `analytic_run_selection` `backend/migrations/versions/0008_assessment_selection.py:28-44`; late acceptance test `backend/tests/test_s3_dispatch_assessment_postgres.py:110-143`. | Queue every affected day and neighboring bracket/continuity window through the existing job path; include attestation changes, not just new accepted readings. Append new selections/revisions, preserve exact old IDs and history; idempotent replay and scoped tenant/Site checks. |
+| Matcher `MATCHER_VERSION` and supported write basis `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/finding_episode.py:66,435-479`; selected interval read has window limitations/jobs `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_assessment_store.py:559-579`; immutable `finding_revision` `backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:64-105`. | Revise the Finding on reassessment. If a generator-series refusal intersects the former/current episode or cuts its candidate tail, carry `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS` with span and linked assessment/evidence IDs into the new Finding revision. State `SERIES_LIMIT` as the episode end reason (new typed claim field), retaining `OPEN_BOUND` only when independently true. Keep supported candidate spans and historical revisions, but never imply the truncated tail was judged avoidable. |
+| Consequence bridge `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:279-320,347-365`, stored consequence read `backend/assetops_backend/financials/consequence_read.py:149-212`; current amount rendering `frontend/src/sites/FindingConsequence.tsx:369-436,526-562`. | Add a read-derived, versioned `money_eligibility` (new shared projection): `ELIGIBLE`, `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, or `PENDING_REASSESSMENT`, with exact FindingRevision, window assessment/run, source limit/evidence and consequence IDs. It must use the **latest selected window and latest Finding** for current claims; historical/pinned rows stay explicitly recorded/audit only. The S4-02b bridge and Finding money panel consume it: suppress the headline amount and all “current” labels on affected run 1 even if consequence currentness remains `CURRENT PRICED`; show the limit, truthful end reason and exact links. Do not delete or relabel stored 7.5625 USD history. S5-01 consumes this same signal, withholds the amount on both non-eligible states and shows the reason/links. |
+| Schedule seam `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/upstream.py:13,110`, health `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/health.py:152-203`, install gate `tools/schedule-install-gate.py:26-43`. | Wire pending S3/S4 work and stopped-worker/currentness state into eligibility; a stale selected window cannot become `ELIGIBLE`. Keep the installation gate closed until reviewed slice completion. S5-01 still owns its own read/UI integration. |
+
+**Money invariant:** `CURRENT PRICED` is necessary but insufficient. Current amount requires the current selected Finding/window to have no material unresolved generator-series limit, no pending reassessment/match, an exact consequence head for that FindingRevision, and the existing source/currentness checks. Other non-series limitations remain visible under the reviewed financial bound. The signal is computed on every read from persisted lineage, so a late evidence acceptance immediately withholds current money while work is pending. Do not rely on a UI-only predicate.
+
+## Phases, proof and review
+
+| Phase | Build and focused proof | Review gate |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| 1 — source-time contract and reassessment | First obtain Architect disposition on the contract/questions below. Build attestation persistence/input basis, new cohort/model version and affected-window requeue. Focused pure cohort and `backend/tests/test_s3_dispatch_assessment_postgres.py` tests: admission-sequenced run 1; source-sequenced late upload; clock absent, out of validity, overlap, cross-epoch, clock step; old and new run/selection IDs coexist; idempotent replay. | Independent phase review of clock evidence, fail-closed boundaries and append-only history. |
+| 2 — Finding and money surfaces | Build new matcher version, truthful series/end claim, shared eligibility and Financials panel. Focused `backend/tests/test_s4_finding_store_postgres.py`, `backend/tests/test_s4_consequence_read.py` and targeted Finding API/UI tests (new) prove suppressed current headline and exact historical linkage. Extend walkthrough with two modes (new switch/assertions) using `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py:111-126,214-343,365-391` and tick/no-hand-run path `tools/s402d_tick_walkthrough.py:94-146,180-375,386-414`; each uses its tool's private DB prefix and drop command. Assert exact S3 status/limits, Finding revision/end reason, consequence amount/IDs, eligibility, UI headline and S5 contract for both variants. The existing SOURCE_ORDER-only assertions must be updated openly, not treated as run-1 proof. | Fresh independent **slice** review, owner walkthrough, then one full backend suite. Run `tools/check-all.ps1` and registry checker. Full suite: `pytest backend/tests -n 2 --dist loadfile --no-loadscope-reorder -q --durations=30 --junitxml=.pytest-junit.xml` per `backend/TESTING.md:103-105`; start `tools/keep-awake-during-tests.ps1` (`backend/TESTING.md:110-120`), enforce 20-minute 2× cap (`backend/TESTING.md:121-153`), 0 skipped/failed. |
+
+**Owner walkthrough:** on a private Site/DB, show run 1 after late acceptance and tick: revised Finding with series limit and `SERIES_LIMIT` end; Financials has no current amount, links to the recorded 7.5625 USD revision and exact assessments. Reload at desktop and narrow width. Then show on-time-sequenced late upload with valid simulated clock attestation, supported full episode and 15.8125 USD current bound, and a real-source-without-clock case that stays limited/no current amount. Do not install the environment schedule on the owner's demo host before review; use the walkthrough's explicit tick.
+
+## Architect questions — one batch, recommendations pending ruling
+
+1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
+2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
+3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
+4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.
+
+**Out of scope:** the broader owner analysis-input-data backlog (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2176-2178`) still owns per-analytic required inputs, cadence and coverage for simulated and real Sites at W1 exit; it must include clock attestation then. This packet closes the specific late-reading ordering and amount hazard. S4-02d I3 decided-at clock comparand remains its separate evidence-owner carry (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2223-2231`).

+
+**Owner walkthrough:** on a private Site/DB, show run 1 after late acceptance and tick: revised Finding with series limit and `SERIES_LIMIT` end; Financials has no current amount, links to the recorded 7.5625 USD revision and exact assessments. +
+## Architect questions — one batch, recommendations pending ruling
+
+1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
+2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
+3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
+4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.
+

+# LR-01 — late-reading series and money limit
+
+Status: implementation-ready (Planner packet, 2026-10-15; owner selected `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` at 0.20% over `I-action-store`; implementation and independent review pending)
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — the Finding and Financials money panel gain owner-visible limit, end-reason and no-current-amount states. Owner walkthrough below is a merge checkpoint.
+Size: M, two phases; phase 2 review is the slice review. Branch for implementation: `task/LR-01-late-reading-series-and-money-limit`.
+Depends on: registry `I-S2-02`, `I-S3-01`, `I-S4-01`, `I-S4-02b`; built S4-02c consequence work and S4-02d schedule are the operational seam. This is a hard prerequisite of `I-site-overview-read` and schedule installation on demo/walkthrough hosts; real pilot additionally requires validated physical clock semantics and the other registry gates.
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+Resolve `.agent/S4-02c-review.md` F1 and `.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md` late-reading carries L-a–L-f, L1 and the S4-02b panel residual under decision `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`. Run 1 currently yields S3 `INDETERMINATE` / `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` / `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS`, a revised `SUPPORTED` Finding ending at 07:00 with only `OPEN_BOUND`, then `CURRENT PRICED` 7.5625 USD. The on-time-sequenced late upload yields 15.8125 USD. Neither figure is an automatic current-money claim when its generator series is unresolved. Do not rewrite the S4-02c fixture or its accepted consequence history.
+
+## Reviewed contract first — proposed S3 cohort v4
+
+Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
+
+1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
+3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.
+
+## Existing code and required changes
+
+| Existing main anchor | Work in this packet |
+| --- | --- |

No Lab private truth as product proof. |
+| `AcceptedReading`, `EvidenceQuery` and input digest `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assessment_inputs.py:70-136,205,334`; Postgres query maps current fields `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_assessment_store.py:48-56,123-136`. | Add attestation provenance/uncertainty to the accepted public analytic input and digest (new fields); avoid retroactively changing the bytes of old runs. |

+| `DispatchAssessmentWorker.reconcile` sees model staleness `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assessment_worker.py:173-185`; immutable `analytic_run_selection` `backend/migrations/versions/0008_assessment_selection.py:28-44`; late acceptance test `backend/tests/test_s3_dispatch_assessment_postgres.py:110-143`. | Queue every affected day and neighboring bracket/continuity window through the existing job path; include attestation changes, not just new accepted readings. +| Matcher `MATCHER_VERSION` and supported write basis `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/finding_episode.py:66,435-479`; selected interval read has window limitations/jobs `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_assessment_store.py:559-579`; immutable `finding_revision` `backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:64-105`. | Revise the Finding on reassessment. +| Consequence bridge `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:279-320,347-365`, stored consequence read `backend/assetops_backend/financials/consequence_read.py:149-212`; current amount rendering `frontend/src/sites/FindingConsequence.tsx:369-436,526-562`. | Add a read-derived, versioned `money_eligibility` (new shared projection): `ELIGIBLE`, `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, or `PENDING_REASSESSMENT`, with exact FindingRevision, window assessment/run, source limit/evidence and consequence IDs. It must use the **latest selected window and latest Finding** for current claims; historical/pinned rows stay explicitly recorded/audit only. The S4-02b bridge and Finding money panel consume it: suppress the headline amount and all “current” labels on affected run 1 even if consequence currentness remains `CURRENT PRICED`; show the limit, truthful end reason and exact links. Keep the installation gate closed until reviewed slice completion. S5-01 still owns its own read/UI integration. |
+
+**Money invariant:** `CURRENT PRICED` is necessary but insufficient. Current amount requires the current selected Finding/window to have no material unresolved generator-series limit, no pending reassessment/match, an exact consequence head for that FindingRevision, and the existing source/currentness checks. Other non-series limitations remain visible under the reviewed financial bound. The signal is computed on every read from persisted lineage, so a late evidence acceptance immediately withholds current money while work is pending. Do not rely on a UI-only predicate.
+
+## Phases, proof and review
+
+| Phase | Build and focused proof | Review gate |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| 1 — source-time contract and reassessment | First obtain Architect disposition on the contract/questions below. +| 2 — Finding and money surfaces | Build new matcher version, truthful series/end claim, shared eligibility and Financials panel. Focused `backend/tests/test_s4_finding_store_postgres.py`, `backend/tests/test_s4_consequence_read.py` and targeted Finding API/UI tests (new) prove suppressed current headline and exact historical linkage. Extend walkthrough with two modes (new switch/assertions) using `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py:111-126,214-343,365-391` and tick/no-hand-run path `tools/s402d_tick_walkthrough.py:94-146,180-375,386-414`; each uses its tool's private DB prefix and drop command. +
+**Owner walkthrough:** on a private Site/DB, show run 1 after late acceptance and tick: revised Finding with series limit and `SERIES_LIMIT` end; Financials has no current amount, links to the recorded 7.5625 USD revision and exact assessments. +
+## Architect questions — one batch, recommendations pending ruling
+
+1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
+2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
+3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
+4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.
+
+**Out of scope:** the broader owner analysis-input-data backlog (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2176-2178`) still owns per-analytic required inputs, cadence and coverage for simulated and real Sites at W1 exit; it must include clock attestation then. This packet closes the specific late-reading ordering and amount hazard. S4-02d I3 decided-at clock comparand remains its separate evidence-owner carry (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2223-2231`).

+# LR-01 — late-reading series and money limit
+
+Status: implementation-ready (Planner packet, 2026-10-15; owner selected `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` at 0.20% over `I-action-store`; implementation and independent review pending)
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — the Finding and Financials money panel gain owner-visible limit, end-reason and no-current-amount states. Owner walkthrough below is a merge checkpoint.
+Size: M, two phases; phase 2 review is the slice review. Branch for implementation: `task/LR-01-late-reading-series-and-money-limit`.
+Depends on: registry `I-S2-02`, `I-S3-01`, `I-S4-01`, `I-S4-02b`; built S4-02c consequence work and S4-02d schedule are the operational seam. This is a hard prerequisite of `I-site-overview-read` and schedule installation on demo/walkthrough hosts; real pilot additionally requires validated physical clock semantics and the other registry gates.
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+Resolve `.agent/S4-02c-review.md` F1 and `.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md` late-reading carries L-a–L-f, L1 and the S4-02b panel residual under decision `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`. Run 1 currently yields S3 `INDETERMINATE` / `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` / `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS`, a revised `SUPPORTED` Finding ending at 07:00 with only `OPEN_BOUND`, then `CURRENT PRICED` 7.5625 USD. The on-time-sequenced late upload yields 15.8125 USD. Neither figure is an automatic current-money claim when its generator series is unresolved. Do not rewrite the S4-02c fixture or its accepted consequence history.
+
+## Reviewed contract first — proposed S3 cohort v4
+
+Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
+
+1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
+3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.
+

+
+**Money invariant:** `CURRENT PRICED` is necessary but insufficient. Current amount requires the current selected Finding/window to have no material unresolved generator-series limit, no pending reassessment/match, an exact consequence head for that FindingRevision, and the existing source/currentness checks. Other non-series limitations remain visible under the reviewed financial bound. The signal is computed on every read from persisted lineage, so a late evidence acceptance immediately withholds current money while work is pending. Do not rely on a UI-only predicate.

+
+## Architect questions — one batch, recommendations pending ruling
+
+1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
+2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
+3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
+4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.
+
+**Out of scope:** the broader owner analysis-input-data backlog (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2176-2178`) still owns per-analytic required inputs, cadence and coverage for simulated and real Sites at W1 exit; it must include clock attestation then. This packet closes the specific late-reading ordering and amount hazard. S4-02d I3 decided-at clock comparand remains its separate evidence-owner carry (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2223-2231`).

+# LR-01 — late-reading series and money limit
+
+Status: implementation-ready (Planner packet, 2026-10-15; owner selected `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` at 0.20% over `I-action-store`; implementation and independent review pending)
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — the Finding and Financials money panel gain owner-visible limit, end-reason and no-current-amount states. Owner walkthrough below is a merge checkpoint.
+Size: M, two phases; phase 2 review is the slice review. Branch for implementation: `task/LR-01-late-reading-series-and-money-limit`.
+Depends on: registry `I-S2-02`, `I-S3-01`, `I-S4-01`, `I-S4-02b`; built S4-02c consequence work and S4-02d schedule are the operational seam. This is a hard prerequisite of `I-site-overview-read` and schedule installation on demo/walkthrough hosts; real pilot additionally requires validated physical clock semantics and the other registry gates.
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+Resolve `.agent/S4-02c-review.md` F1 and `.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md` late-reading carries L-a–L-f, L1 and the S4-02b panel residual under decision `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`. Run 1 currently yields S3 `INDETERMINATE` / `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` / `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS`, a revised `SUPPORTED` Finding ending at 07:00 with only `OPEN_BOUND`, then `CURRENT PRICED` 7.5625 USD. The on-time-sequenced late upload yields 15.8125 USD. Neither figure is an automatic current-money claim when its generator series is unresolved. Do not rewrite the S4-02c fixture or its accepted consequence history.
+
+## Reviewed contract first — proposed S3 cohort v4
+
+Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
+
+1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
+3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.
+
+## Existing code and required changes
+
+| Existing main anchor | Work in this packet |
+| --- | --- |
+| Gateway increments sequence on admission `gateway/assetops_gateway/service.py:8-11,649-655`; accepted rows contain `source_time`, `sequence_epoch`, `sequence`, `source_mode` but no clock attestation `backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:161-207`. | Add a versioned public clock-attestation record and a reviewed ingestion/fixture route (new migration/adapter/API or importer); preserve an explicit unavailable state for unvalidated real sources. No Lab private truth as product proof. |
+| `AcceptedReading`, `EvidenceQuery` and input digest `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assessment_inputs.py:70-136,205,334`; Postgres query maps current fields `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_assessment_store.py:48-56,123-136`. | Add attestation provenance/uncertainty to the accepted public analytic input and digest (new fields); avoid retroactively changing the bytes of old runs. |

Cover clock absent/expired/conflicting, overlap, epoch switch, run 1 and sequenced-on-time upload. Continue typed `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS` at window level where refused. |

It must use the **latest selected window and latest Finding** for current claims; historical/pinned rows stay explicitly recorded/audit only. The S4-02b bridge and Finding money panel consume it: suppress the headline amount and all “current” labels on affected run 1 even if consequence currentness remains `CURRENT PRICED`; show the limit, truthful end reason and exact links. Do not delete or relabel stored 7.5625 USD history. S5-01 consumes this same signal, withholds the amount on both non-eligible states and shows the reason/links. |
+| Schedule seam `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/upstream.py:13,110`, health `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/health.py:152-203`, install gate `tools/schedule-install-gate.py:26-43`. | Wire pending S3/S4 work and stopped-worker/currentness state into eligibility; a stale selected window cannot become `ELIGIBLE`. Keep the installation gate closed until reviewed slice completion. S5-01 still owns its own read/UI integration. |
+
+**Money invariant:** `CURRENT PRICED` is necessary but insufficient. Current amount requires the current selected Finding/window to have no material unresolved generator-series limit, no pending reassessment/match, an exact consequence head for that FindingRevision, and the existing source/currentness checks. Other non-series limitations remain visible under the reviewed financial bound. The signal is computed on every read from persisted lineage, so a late evidence acceptance immediately withholds current money while work is pending. Do not rely on a UI-only predicate.
+

+

+
+**Owner walkthrough:** on a private Site/DB, show run 1 after late acceptance and tick: revised Finding with series limit and `SERIES_LIMIT` end; Financials has no current amount, links to the recorded 7.5625 USD revision and exact assessments. Reload at desktop and narrow width. Do not install the environment schedule on the owner's demo host before review; use the walkthrough's explicit tick.
+
+## Architect questions — one batch, recommendations pending ruling
+
+1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
+2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
+3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
+4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.
+
+**Out of scope:** the broader owner analysis-input-data backlog (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2176-2178`) still owns per-analytic required inputs, cadence and coverage for simulated and real Sites at W1 exit; it must include clock attestation then. This packet closes the specific late-reading ordering and amount hazard. S4-02d I3 decided-at clock comparand remains its separate evidence-owner carry (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2223-2231`).

+# LR-01 — late-reading series and money limit
+
+Status: implementation-ready (Planner packet, 2026-10-15; owner selected `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` at 0.20% over `I-action-store`; implementation and independent review pending)
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — the Finding and Financials money panel gain owner-visible limit, end-reason and no-current-amount states. Owner walkthrough below is a merge checkpoint.
+Size: M, two phases; phase 2 review is the slice review. Branch for implementation: `task/LR-01-late-reading-series-and-money-limit`.
+Depends on: registry `I-S2-02`, `I-S3-01`, `I-S4-01`, `I-S4-02b`; built S4-02c consequence work and S4-02d schedule are the operational seam. This is a hard prerequisite of `I-site-overview-read` and schedule installation on demo/walkthrough hosts; real pilot additionally requires validated physical clock semantics and the other registry gates.
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+Resolve `.agent/S4-02c-review.md` F1 and `.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md` late-reading carries L-a–L-f, L1 and the S4-02b panel residual under decision `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`. Run 1 currently yields S3 `INDETERMINATE` / `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` / `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS`, a revised `SUPPORTED` Finding ending at 07:00 with only `OPEN_BOUND`, then `CURRENT PRICED` 7.5625 USD. The on-time-sequenced late upload yields 15.8125 USD. Neither figure is an automatic current-money claim when its generator series is unresolved. Do not rewrite the S4-02c fixture or its accepted consequence history.
+
+## Reviewed contract first — proposed S3 cohort v4
+
+Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
+
+1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
+3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.
+
+## Existing code and required changes
+
+| Existing main anchor | Work in this packet |
+| --- | --- |

It must use the **latest selected window and latest Finding** for current claims; historical/pinned rows stay explicitly recorded/audit only. The S4-02b bridge and Finding money panel consume it: suppress the headline amount and all “current” labels on affected run 1 even if consequence currentness remains `CURRENT PRICED`; show the limit, truthful end reason and exact links. Do not delete or relabel stored 7.5625 USD history. S5-01 consumes this same signal, withholds the amount on both non-eligible states and shows the reason/links. |
+| Schedule seam `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/upstream.py:13,110`, health `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/health.py:152-203`, install gate `tools/schedule-install-gate.py:26-43`. | Wire pending S3/S4 work and stopped-worker/currentness state into eligibility; a stale selected window cannot become `ELIGIBLE`. Keep the installation gate closed until reviewed slice completion. S5-01 still owns its own read/UI integration. |
+
+**Money invariant:** `CURRENT PRICED` is necessary but insufficient. Current amount requires the current selected Finding/window to have no material unresolved generator-series limit, no pending reassessment/match, an exact consequence head for that FindingRevision, and the existing source/currentness checks. Other non-series limitations remain visible under the reviewed financial bound. The signal is computed on every read from persisted lineage, so a late evidence acceptance immediately withholds current money while work is pending. Do not rely on a UI-only predicate.
+
+## Phases, proof and review
+
+| Phase | Build and focused proof | Review gate |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| 1 — source-time contract and reassessment | First obtain Architect disposition on the contract/questions below. +| 2 — Finding and money surfaces | Build new matcher version, truthful series/end claim, shared eligibility and Financials panel. Focused `backend/tests/test_s4_finding_store_postgres.py`, `backend/tests/test_s4_consequence_read.py` and targeted Finding API/UI tests (new) prove suppressed current headline and exact historical linkage. +## Architect questions — one batch, recommendations pending ruling
+
+1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
+2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
+3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
+4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.

+# LR-01 — late-reading series and money limit
+
+Status: implementation-ready (Planner packet, 2026-10-15; owner selected `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` at 0.20% over `I-action-store`; implementation and independent review pending)
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — the Finding and Financials money panel gain owner-visible limit, end-reason and no-current-amount states. Owner walkthrough below is a merge checkpoint.
+Size: M, two phases; phase 2 review is the slice review. Branch for implementation: `task/LR-01-late-reading-series-and-money-limit`.
+Depends on: registry `I-S2-02`, `I-S3-01`, `I-S4-01`, `I-S4-02b`; built S4-02c consequence work and S4-02d schedule are the operational seam. This is a hard prerequisite of `I-site-overview-read` and schedule installation on demo/walkthrough hosts; real pilot additionally requires validated physical clock semantics and the other registry gates.
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+Resolve `.agent/S4-02c-review.md` F1 and `.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md` late-reading carries L-a–L-f, L1 and the S4-02b panel residual under decision `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`. Run 1 currently yields S3 `INDETERMINATE` / `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` / `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS`, a revised `SUPPORTED` Finding ending at 07:00 with only `OPEN_BOUND`, then `CURRENT PRICED` 7.5625 USD. The on-time-sequenced late upload yields 15.8125 USD. Neither figure is an automatic current-money claim when its generator series is unresolved. Do not rewrite the S4-02c fixture or its accepted consequence history.
+
+## Reviewed contract first — proposed S3 cohort v4
+
+Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
+
+1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
+3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.
+

+
+**Money invariant:** `CURRENT PRICED` is necessary but insufficient. Current amount requires the current selected Finding/window to have no material unresolved generator-series limit, no pending reassessment/match, an exact consequence head for that FindingRevision, and the existing source/currentness checks. Other non-series limitations remain visible under the reviewed financial bound. The signal is computed on every read from persisted lineage, so a late evidence acceptance immediately withholds current money while work is pending. Do not rely on a UI-only predicate.

+
+## Architect questions — one batch, recommendations pending ruling
+
+1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
+2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
+3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
+4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.
+
+**Out of scope:** the broader owner analysis-input-data backlog (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2176-2178`) still owns per-analytic required inputs, cadence and coverage for simulated and real Sites at W1 exit; it must include clock attestation then. This packet closes the specific late-reading ordering and amount hazard. S4-02d I3 decided-at clock comparand remains its separate evidence-owner carry (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2223-2231`).

+# LR-01 — late-reading series and money limit
+
+Status: implementation-ready (Planner packet, 2026-10-15; owner selected `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` at 0.20% over `I-action-store`; implementation and independent review pending)
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — the Finding and Financials money panel gain owner-visible limit, end-reason and no-current-amount states. Owner walkthrough below is a merge checkpoint.
+Size: M, two phases; phase 2 review is the slice review. Branch for implementation: `task/LR-01-late-reading-series-and-money-limit`.
+Depends on: registry `I-S2-02`, `I-S3-01`, `I-S4-01`, `I-S4-02b`; built S4-02c consequence work and S4-02d schedule are the operational seam. This is a hard prerequisite of `I-site-overview-read` and schedule installation on demo/walkthrough hosts; real pilot additionally requires validated physical clock semantics and the other registry gates.
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+Resolve `.agent/S4-02c-review.md` F1 and `.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md` late-reading carries L-a–L-f, L1 and the S4-02b panel residual under decision `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`. Run 1 currently yields S3 `INDETERMINATE` / `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` / `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS`, a revised `SUPPORTED` Finding ending at 07:00 with only `OPEN_BOUND`, then `CURRENT PRICED` 7.5625 USD. The on-time-sequenced late upload yields 15.8125 USD. Neither figure is an automatic current-money claim when its generator series is unresolved. Keep the existing authored fixture `backend/conversions/s4-power-late-demo-cases-v1.json:2-13` and accepted consequence history.
+
+## Reviewed contract first — proposed S3 cohort v4
+
+Architect review of this contract precedes implementation. The recommendation is deterministic and fail closed:
+
+1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is ≤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone never proves a real clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
+2. Treat each reading as the source-time uncertainty interval `[source_time−error, source_time+error]` (or the reported measurement interval widened by error). Strictly disjoint intervals may be ordered independent of gateway admission sequence. Equal or overlapping intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous; do not select a winner or silently interpolate. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning two conflicting clock attestations remains limited. A 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not an assertion that the current gateway meets it.
+3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit-selection limits remain; a new continuity proof would need separate review. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.
+
+## Existing code and required changes
+
+| Existing main anchor | Work in this packet |
+| --- | --- |

No Lab private truth as product proof. |

It must use the **latest selected window and latest Finding** for current claims; historical/pinned rows stay explicitly recorded/audit only. The S4-02b bridge and Finding money panel consume it: suppress the headline amount and all “current” labels on affected run 1 even if consequence currentness remains `CURRENT PRICED`; show the limit, truthful end reason and exact links. Do not delete or relabel stored 7.5625 USD history. S5-01 consumes this same signal, withholds the amount on both non-eligible states and shows the reason/links. |
+| Schedule seam `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/upstream.py:13,110`, health `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/health.py:152-203`, install gate `tools/schedule-install-gate.py:26-43`. | Wire pending S3/S4 work and stopped-worker/currentness state into eligibility; a stale selected window cannot become `ELIGIBLE`. Keep the installation gate closed until reviewed slice completion. S5-01 still owns its own read/UI integration. |
+
+**Money invariant:** `CURRENT PRICED` is necessary but insufficient. Current amount requires the current selected Finding/window to have no material unresolved generator-series limit, no pending reassessment/match, an exact consequence head for that FindingRevision, and the existing source/currentness checks. Other non-series limitations remain visible under the reviewed financial bound. The signal is computed on every read from persisted lineage, so a late evidence acceptance immediately withholds current money while work is pending. Do not rely on a UI-only predicate.
+

+**Owner walkthrough:** on a private Site/DB, show run 1 after late acceptance and tick: revised Finding with series limit and `SERIES_LIMIT` end; Financials has no current amount, links to the recorded 7.5625 USD revision and exact assessments. Do not install the environment schedule on the owner's demo host before review; use the walkthrough's explicit tick.
+
+## Architect questions — one batch, recommendations pending ruling
+
+1. **Clock proof and limit:** Is a durable per-source/epoch attestation with ≤60-second error, validity interval, authority and explicit simulated provenance sufficient? **Recommend yes**, with source-time intervals widened by error and unvalidated real sources limited. If the threshold/authority differs, change the contract, schema and fixtures before phase 1.
+2. **Overlap and epoch:** Must overlapping uncertainty intervals remain ambiguous, and must a new epoch require explicit selection/continuity proof? **Recommend yes**. If a continuity proof is permitted, define its public provenance before implementation; never infer it from time alone.
+3. **Affected-window scope:** On clock-attestation change, recompute every day whose six-hour evidence bracket intersects affected source times plus neighboring continuity days? **Recommend yes**, using existing job and immutable selection paths. If the bracket changes, revise the query/requeue bound together.
+4. **Finding and eligibility form:** Should the revised Finding retain `SUPPORTED` for proven Candidate spans, add typed `SERIES_LIMIT` end and limitation, and expose the read-derived three-state `money_eligibility` to both S4 Financials and S5? **Recommend yes**. If support must instead become indeterminate, change matcher acceptance and both UI states, while preserving the no-current-amount invariant.
+
+**Out of scope:** the broader owner analysis-input-data backlog (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2176-2178`) still owns per-analytic required inputs, cadence and coverage for simulated and real Sites at W1 exit; it must include clock attestation then. This packet closes the specific late-reading ordering and amount hazard. S4-02d I3 decided-at clock comparand remains its separate evidence-owner carry (`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2223-2231`).
